Sebelumnya aplikasi hanya punya \u003cstrong\u003esatu\u003c/strong\u003e cara menghitung harga:\n\u003ccode\u003eharga product + harga tambahan varian\u003c/code\u003e, dikali \u003ccode\u003eqty\u003c/code\u003e. Sekarang setiap product\n\u003cstrong\u003emendefinisikan sendiri\u003c/strong\u003e cara harganya dihitung lewat konfigurasi — bukan\nlewat nama product.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eModel/domain: \u003ca href=\"/MhdFarhannn/Katalog-Percetakan/blob/main/Models/Pricing.cs\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003eModels/Pricing.cs\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eKalkulasi (sumber kebenaran): \u003ca href=\"/MhdFarhannn/Katalog-Percetakan/blob/main/Services/PricingCalculator.cs\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003eServices/PricingCalculator.cs\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003ePembacaan konfigurasi dari DB: \u003ca href=\"/MhdFarhannn/Katalog-Percetakan/blob/main/Services/PricingServices.cs\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003eServices/PricingServices.cs\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eMigrasi database: \u003ca href=\"/MhdFarhannn/Katalog-Percetakan/blob/main/Schema/migration_pricing_modes.sql\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003eSchema/migration_pricing_modes.sql\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eTes: \u003ca href=\"/MhdFarhannn/Katalog-Percetakan/blob/main/tests/Percetakan.Tests/PricingCalculatorTests.cs\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003etests/Percetakan.Tests/PricingCalculatorTests.cs\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e1. Mengapa Banyak Pricing Mode Dibutuhkan\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-1-mengapa-banyak-pricing-mode-dibutuhkan\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 1. 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eProduct percetakan tidak semuanya dihargai dengan cara yang sama:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cmarkdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\u003ctable\u003e\n\u003cthead\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eProduct\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eCara harga wajar\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/thead\u003e\n\u003ctbody\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eSpanduk / Banner\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eper luas (m²), karena ukuran bebas\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eStanding Banner\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eharga tetap per unit, ukuran sudah ditentukan\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eBusiness Card\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eper unit / kuantitas\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eSticker\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003ebisa per luas\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eKain / Fabric\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003ebisa per meter lurus\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eBrosur\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eper kuantitas\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/tbody\u003e\n\u003c/table\u003e\u003c/markdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eMemaksa semua product memakai satu rumus (mis. per meter) membuat product\nfixed-size salah harga, dan menebak perilaku dari \u003ccode\u003eproduct.nama\u003c/code\u003e rapuh:\nmengganti nama product akan mengubah harga. Karena itu perilaku harga\ndijadikan \u003cstrong\u003ekonfigurasi per product\u003c/strong\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cblockquote\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eAturan penting:\u003c/strong\u003e jangan pernah menulis \u003ccode\u003eif (product.Nama == \"Banner\")\u003c/code\u003e.\nPerilaku harga hanya boleh berasal dari \u003ccode\u003eproduct.pricing_mode\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/blockquote\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e2. lebar_cm\n        ▼\nServices/PricingCalculator.cs          (satu-satunya rumus + validasi)\n        │\n        ▼\nPricingResult  →  Pesanan_Detail (snapshot)  +  Pesanan.total_harga\"\u003e\u003cpre class=\"notranslate\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003eCustomer input (width/height/length/qty)\n        │\n        ▼\nController/PesananController.cs        (parsing + validasi awal)\n        │\n        ▼\nServices/PricingServices.cs            (baca konfigurasi dari DB)\n        │  product.pricing_mode / harga / dimension_unit\n        │  Ukuran_Produk.harga / harga_tambahan / panjang_cm / lebar_cm\n        ▼\nServices/PricingCalculator.cs          (satu-satunya rumus + validasi)\n        │\n        ▼\nPricingResult  →  Pesanan_Detail (snapshot)  +  Pesanan.total_harga\n\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eBackend adalah sumber kebenaran.\u003c/strong\u003e Client tidak pernah mengirim harga.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eSatu jalur perhitungan\u003c/strong\u003e (\u003ccode\u003ePricingCalculator\u003c/code\u003e), dipakai untuk validasi\n(controller) dan untuk menyimpan pesanan (dalam transaksi DB).\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" dimensi\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eHarga\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/thead\u003e\n\u003ctbody\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eFixed\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eharga qty\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003ePerUnit\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eharga per aturan khusus\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003esaat ini seperti \u003ccode\u003eFixed\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/tbody\u003e\n\u003c/table\u003e\u003c/markdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\n\u003cp href=\"#18-cara-menambah-pricing-mode-baru\"\u003e§18\u003c/a\u003e).\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" Product vs Product Variant\" href=\"#6-product-vs-product-variant\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eVarian product direpresentasikan oleh tabel \u003cstrong\u003e\u003ccode\u003eUkuran_Produk\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e (sudah ada\nsebelum perubahan ini). Harga dikonfigurasi di \u003cstrong\u003ekedua level\u003c/strong\u003e dengan aturan\nsederhana dan tunggal:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"rate = varian.harga                       (jika diisi)\n       ?? product.harga + varian.harga_tambahan\"\u003e\u003cpre class=\"notranslate\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003erate = varian.harga                       (jika diisi)\n       ?? product.harga + varian.harga_tambahan\n\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eAlasan:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003ccode\u003eFixed\u003c/code\u003e + ukuran tetap\u003c/strong\u003e (Standing Banner 60×160 vs 80×180) butuh harga\nper varian → pakai \u003ccode\u003eUkuran_Produk.harga\u003c/code\u003e (\u003ccode\u003epanjang_cm\u003c/code\u003e/\u003ccode\u003elebar_cm\u003c/code\u003e sebagai\ndimensi tetap).\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003ccode\u003ePerArea\u003c/code\u003e / \u003ccode\u003ePerLength\u003c/code\u003e / \u003ccode\u003ePerUnit\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e pada dasarnya adalah rate di level\nproduct → cukup \u003ccode\u003eproduct.harga\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eVarian \u003cstrong\u003etanpa\u003c/strong\u003e \u003ccode\u003eUkuran_Produk.harga\u003c/code\u003e tetap memakai \u003ccode\u003eharga_tambahan\u003c/code\u003e,\nsehingga data lama tidak perlu diubah.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003epricing_mode\u003c/code\u003e \u003cstrong\u003etidak\u003c/strong\u003e disimpan per varian; mode dimiliki product. Bila suatu\nsaat mode perlu berbeda per varian, tambahkan kolom\n\u003ccode\u003eUkuran_Produk.pricing_mode\u003c/code\u003e dan gabungkan di \u003ccode\u003ePricingServices\u003c/code\u003e — kalkulator\ntidak perlu berubah.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e7. (product.harga + Ukuran_Produk.harga_tambahan)\n\nFixed / Custom :  subtotal = rate x qty\nPerUnit        :  subtotal = rate x qty\nPerArea        :  area     = width_m x height_m\n                  subtotal = area x rate x qty\nPerLength      :  subtotal = length_m x rate x qty\n\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eContoh hasil (sesuai tes otomatis):\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cmarkdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\u003ctable\u003e\n\u003cthead\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eMode\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eKonfigurasi\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eInput\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eSubtotal\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/thead\u003e\n\u003ctbody\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eFixed\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003erate 150.000\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eqty 2\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eRp300.000\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003ePerArea\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003erate 25.000/m²\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e3 m × 1 m, qty 2\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eRp150.000\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003ePerLength\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003erate dir=\"auto\"\u003eUang dibulatkan \u003cstrong\u003e2 desimal\u003c/strong\u003e dengan tidak\ndipakai sebagai faktor harga karena harga \u003ccode\u003eFixed\u003c/code\u003e sudah per unit).\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eAlur eksplisit:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"Input customer (satuan product.dimension_unit)\n    ↓ validasi (\u0026gt; Field baru per\nitem (opsional, dipakai sesuai mode):\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cmarkdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\u003ctable\u003e\n\u003cthead\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eForm field\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eTipe\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eDipakai oleh\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/thead\u003e\n\u003ctbody\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eitems[N].width\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003edecimal\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003ePerArea\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eitems[N].height\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003edecimal\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003ePerArea\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eitems[N].length\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003edecimal\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003ePerLength\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/tbody\u003e\n\u003c/table\u003e\u003c/markdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003eitems[N].qty\u003c/code\u003e selalu wajib. Satuan \u003ccode\u003ewidth\u003c/code\u003e/\u003ccode\u003eheight\u003c/code\u003e/\u003ccode\u003elength\u003c/code\u003e mengikuti\n\u003ccode\u003eproduct.dimensionUnit\u003c/code\u003e. \u003cstrong\u003eTidak ada\u003c/strong\u003e field harga yang diterima; bila client\nmengirim \u003ccode\u003eprice\u003c/code\u003e/\u003ccode\u003eharga\u003c/code\u003e, field itu diabaikan total.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eContoh \u003ccode\u003ePerArea\u003c/code\u003e:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"idAlamat: 5\nitems[0].idProduct: 1\nitems[0].width: 3\nitems[0].height: 1\nitems[0].qty: 2\"\u003e\u003cpre class=\"notranslate\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003eidAlamat: 5\nitems[0].idProduct: 1\nitems[0].width: 3\nitems[0].height: 1\nitems[0].qty: 2\n\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eContoh \u003ccode\u003eFixed\u003c/code\u003e (Standing Banner, varian 60×160):\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"idAlamat: 5\nitems[0].idProduct: 7\nitems[0].idUkuranProduk: 3\nitems[0].qty: 2\"\u003e\u003cpre class=\"notranslate\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003eidAlamat: 5\nitems[0].idProduct: 7\nitems[0].idUkuranProduk: 3\nitems[0].qty: 2\n\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003ePesananDetailResponse\u003c/code\u003e menambahkan \u003ccode\u003epricingMode\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003ewidthMeters\u003c/code\u003e,\n\u003ccode\u003eheightMeters\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003elengthMeters\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003edimensionUnit\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eareaM2\u003c/code\u003e, dan \u003ccode\u003esubtotal\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e11. Perilaku Cart\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-11-perilaku-cart\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 11. Perilaku Cart\" href=\"#11-perilaku-cart\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eRepo ini \u003cstrong\u003etidak memiliki cart/keranjang di server\u003c/strong\u003e. Item \"cart\" hidup di\nclient dan dikirim langsung sebagai \u003ccode\u003eitems[]\u003c/code\u003e pada \u003ccode\u003ePOST /api/v1/pesanan\u003c/code\u003e.\nKarena itu tidak ada tabel cart baru.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eData minimum per item yang harus disimpan/dikirim client:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003eidProduct\u003c/code\u003e (wajib)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003eidUkuranProduk\u003c/code\u003e (opsional, untuk varian)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003eqty\u003c/code\u003e (wajib)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003ewidth\u003c/code\u003e / \u003ccode\u003eheight\u003c/code\u003e / \u003ccode\u003elength\u003c/code\u003e (sesuai mode)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003esubtotal\u003c/code\u003e/\u003ccode\u003earea\u003c/code\u003e \u003cstrong\u003etidak\u003c/strong\u003e disimpan di cart karena selalu bisa dihitung ulang.\nClient boleh menghitung estimasi untuk UI, tetapi server selalu menghitung\nulang harga final.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e12. Perilaku Order\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-12-perilaku-order\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 12. Perilaku Order\" href=\"#12-perilaku-order\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003ePesananServices.CreatePesananAsync\u003c/code\u003e / \u003ccode\u003eUpdatePesananAsync\u003c/code\u003e:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003col dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003emembuka transaksi,\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003euntuk setiap item memanggil \u003ccode\u003ePricingServices.EvaluateAsync(conn, tx, input)\u003c/code\u003e,\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003ebila product/varian tidak valid atau validasi gagal → rollback, \u003ccode\u003e400\u003c/code\u003e,\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003emenjumlahkan \u003ccode\u003ePricingResult.Subtotal\u003c/code\u003e ke \u003ccode\u003ePesanan.total_harga\u003c/code\u003e,\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003emenulis snapshot ke \u003ccode\u003ePesanan_Detail\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003eUpdatePesananAsync\u003c/code\u003e mengganti seluruh detail (detail lama di-soft delete),\ntetap menghitung ulang harga dari konfigurasi saat itu.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e13. Preservasi Harga Historis\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-13-preservasi-harga-historis\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 13. Preservasi Harga Historis\" href=\"#13-preservasi-harga-historis\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eHarga pesanan lama tidak boleh berubah bila harga product diubah:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003ePesanan_Detail.harga_satuan\u003c/code\u003e menyimpan \u003cstrong\u003erate\u003c/strong\u003e snapshot.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003ePesanan_Detail.subtotal\u003c/code\u003e menyimpan \u003cstrong\u003esubtotal\u003c/strong\u003e snapshot (bukan dihitung\nulang dari \u003ccode\u003eproduct.harga\u003c/code\u003e saat dibaca).\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003ewidth_m\u003c/code\u003e/\u003ccode\u003eheight_m\u003c/code\u003e/\u003ccode\u003elength_m\u003c/code\u003e dan \u003ccode\u003epricing_mode\u003c/code\u003e menyimpan konteks\nperhitungan.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003ePesanan.total_harga\u003c/code\u003e adalah snapshot total.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eRead query memakai \u003ccode\u003eCOALESCE(d.subtotal, d.harga_satuan * d.qty)\u003c/code\u003e sehingga baris\nlama (sebelum migrasi) tetap benar. \u003ccode\u003eareaM2\u003c/code\u003e dihitung ulang saat query\n(\u003ccode\u003ewidth_m * height_m\u003c/code\u003e) karena tidak perlu disimpan.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e14. Migrasi Database\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-14-migrasi-database\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 14. Migrasi Database\" href=\"#14-migrasi-database\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eFresh install: \u003ca href=\"/MhdFarhannn/Katalog-Percetakan/blob/main/Schema/setup.sql\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003eSchema/setup.sql\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/a\u003e sudah memuat kolom baru.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eDatabase berjalan:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"mysql -u \u0026lt;user\u0026gt; -p katalog_percetakan \u0026lt; Schema/migration_pricing_modes.sql\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003emysql -u \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\u0026lt;\u003c/span\u003euser\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\u0026gt;\u003c/span\u003e -p katalog_percetakan \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\u0026lt;\u003c/span\u003e Schema/migration_pricing_modes.sql\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eMigrasi bersifat \u003cstrong\u003eidempotent\u003c/strong\u003e (dicek lewat \u003ccode\u003einformation_schema\u003c/code\u003e) dan \u003cstrong\u003etidak\ndestruktif\u003c/strong\u003e:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eMenambah 2 kolom \u003ccode\u003eproduct\u003c/code\u003e, 3 kolom \u003ccode\u003eUkuran_Produk\u003c/code\u003e, 6 kolom\n\u003ccode\u003ePesanan_Detail\u003c/code\u003e (semua dengan \u003ccode\u003eDEFAULT\u003c/code\u003e / \u003ccode\u003eNULL\u003c/code\u003e).\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eBackfill aman:\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003ePesanan_Detail.pricing_mode = 'Fixed'\u003c/code\u003e bila NULL,\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003ePesanan_Detail.dimension_unit = 'meter'\u003c/code\u003e bila NULL,\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003ePesanan_Detail.subtotal = harga_satuan * qty\u003c/code\u003e bila NULL.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eTidak ada kolom lama yang dihapus.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e15. Kompatibilitas Mundur (Backward Compatibility)\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-15-kompatibilitas-mundur-backward-compatibility\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 15. Kompatibilitas Mundur (Backward Compatibility)\" href=\"#15-kompatibilitas-mundur-backward-compatibility\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eProduct lama\u003c/strong\u003e otomatis \u003ccode\u003epricing_mode = 'Fixed'\u003c/code\u003e dan\n\u003ccode\u003edimension_unit = 'meter'\u003c/code\u003e; rumusnya identik dengan sebelumnya\n(\u003ccode\u003eharga + harga_tambahan\u003c/code\u003e) × qty.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eVarian lama\u003c/strong\u003e (\u003ccode\u003eUkuran_Produk.harga\u003c/code\u003e NULL) tetap memakai \u003ccode\u003eharga_tambahan\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eKlien lama\u003c/strong\u003e yang hanya mengirim \u003ccode\u003eidProduct\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eidUkuranProduk\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eqty\u003c/code\u003e\ntetap bekerja untuk product \u003ccode\u003eFixed\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003ePesanan lama\u003c/strong\u003e tetap terbaca; \u003ccode\u003esubtotal\u003c/code\u003e di-backfill.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eProduct yang ingin memakai \u003ccode\u003ePerArea\u003c/code\u003e/\u003ccode\u003ePerLength\u003c/code\u003e/\u003ccode\u003ePerUnit\u003c/code\u003e \u003cstrong\u003eharus\u003c/strong\u003e\ndikonfigurasi manual (lihat \u003ca href=\"#17-cara-menambah-product-baru\"\u003e§17\u003c/a\u003e) — tidak\nditebak otomatis karena salah tebak berarti salah harga.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e16. CalculatePerVolume(...)\u003c/code\u003e).\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eBila butuh input baru, tambahkan field di \u003ccode\u003ePricingInput\u003c/code\u003e dan (bila perlu\ndikirim client) pada \u003ccode\u003ePesananDetailRequest\u003c/code\u003e + parsing multipart.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eTambahkan test di \u003ccode\u003etests/Percetakan.Tests/\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eTidak perlu migrasi\u003c/strong\u003e untuk kolom mode (tersimpan sebagai string).\nTambah kolom hanya bila perlu menyimpan data baru.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eContoh kerangka:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-cs notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"private static PricingResult CalculatePerVolume(\n    PricingConfiguration config,\n    PricingInput input) { /* rumus baru */ }\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eprivate\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003estatic\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003ePricingResult\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003eCalculatePerVolume\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e(\u003c/span\u003e\n    \u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003ePricingConfiguration\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003econfig\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e,\u003c/span\u003e\n    \u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003ePricingInput\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003einput\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e)\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e{\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e/* rumus baru */\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e}\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e19. changes","ghDesktopPath":"https://desktop.github.com","isGitLfs":false,"onBranch":true,"shortPath":"14097b9","siteNavLoginPath":"/login?return_to=https%3A%2F%2Fgithub.com%2FMhdFarhannn%2FKatalog-Percetakan%2Fblob%2Fmain%2Fdocs%2Fpricing-system.md","isCSV":false,"isRichtext":true,"lineInfo":{"truncatedLoc":"502","truncatedSloc":"390"},"mode":"file"},"image":false,"isCodeownersFile":null,"isPlain":false,"isValidLegacyIssueTemplate":false,"isIssueTemplate":false,"isDiscussionTemplate":false,"language":"Markdown","languageID":222,"large":false,"planSupportInfo":{"repoIsFork":null,"repoOwnedByCurrentUser":null,"requestFullPath":"/MhdFarhannn/Katalog-Percetakan/blob/main/docs/pricing-system.md","showFreeOrgGatedFeatureMessage":null,"showPlanSupportBanner":null,"upgradeDataAttributes":null,"upgradePath":null},"publishBannersInfo":{"dismissActionNoticePath":"/settings/dismiss-notice/publish_action_from_dockerfile","releasePath":"/MhdFarhannn/Katalog-Percetakan/releases/new?marketplace=true","showPublishActionBanner":false},"rawBlobUrl":"https://github.com/MhdFarhannn/Katalog-Percetakan/raw/refs/heads/main/docs/pricing-system.md","renderImageOrRaw":false,"shortPath":null,"symbolsEnabled":true,"tabSize":4,"topBannersInfo":{"overridingGlobalFundingFile":false,"globalPreferredFundingPath":null,"showInvalidCitationWarning":false,"citationHelpUrl":"https://docs.github.com/github/creating-cloning-and-archiving-repositories/creating-a-repository-on-github/about-citation-files","actionsOnboardingTip":null},"truncated":false,"viewable":true,"workflowRedirectUrl":null},"copilotInfo":null,"copilotAccessAllowed":false,"copilotSpacesEnabled":false,"modelsAccessAllowed":false,"modelsRepoIntegrationEnabled":false,"isMarketplaceEnabled":true},"codeViewBlobLayoutRoute.StyledBlob":{"rawLines":["# Pricing System (Sistem Harga)","","Dokumen ini menjelaskan bagaimana harga product dihitung di Katalog","Percetakan. Sebelumnya aplikasi hanya punya **satu** cara menghitung harga:","`harga product + harga tambahan varian`, dikali `qty`. Sekarang setiap product","**mendefinisikan sendiri** cara harganya dihitung lewat konfigurasi — bukan","lewat nama product.","","- Model/domain: [`Models/Pricing.cs`](../Models/Pricing.cs)","- Kalkulasi (sumber kebenaran): [`Services/PricingCalculator.cs`](../Services/PricingCalculator.cs)","- Pembacaan konfigurasi dari DB: [`Services/PricingServices.cs`](../Services/PricingServices.cs)","- Migrasi database: [`Schema/migration_pricing_modes.sql`](../Schema/migration_pricing_modes.sql)","- Tes: [`tests/Percetakan.Tests/PricingCalculatorTests.cs`](../tests/Percetakan.Tests/PricingCalculatorTests.cs)","","## 1. Mengapa Banyak Pricing Mode Dibutuhkan","","Product percetakan tidak semuanya dihargai dengan cara yang sama:","","| Product | Cara harga wajar |","|---|---|","| Spanduk / Banner | per luas (m²), karena ukuran bebas |","| Standing Banner | harga tetap per unit, ukuran sudah ditentukan |","| Business Card | per unit / kuantitas |","| Sticker | bisa per luas |","| Kain / Fabric | bisa per meter lurus |","| Brosur | per kuantitas |","","Memaksa semua product memakai satu rumus (mis. per meter) membuat product","fixed-size salah harga, dan menebak perilaku dari `product.nama` rapuh:","mengganti nama product akan mengubah harga. Karena itu perilaku harga","dijadikan **konfigurasi per product**.","","\u003e **Aturan penting:** jangan pernah menulis `if (product.Nama == \"Banner\")`.","\u003e Perilaku harga hanya boleh berasal dari `product.pricing_mode`.","","## 2. Arsitektur Harga Sebelumnya","","```","product.harga  +  Ukuran_Produk.harga_tambahan   (jika ukuran dipilih)","        │","        ▼","   harga_satuan  x  qty  =  Pesanan_Detail / Pesanan.total_harga","```","","Semua logika ada di `Services/PesananServices.cs` (`GetHargaSatuanAsync`),","tidak ada validasi dimensi, dan tidak ada cara menyatakan \"harga per m²\".","","## 3. Arsitektur Harga Sekarang","","```","Customer input (width/height/length/qty)","        │","        ▼","Controller/PesananController.cs        (parsing + validasi awal)","        │","        ▼","Services/PricingServices.cs            (baca konfigurasi dari DB)","        │  product.pricing_mode / harga / dimension_unit","        │  Ukuran_Produk.harga / harga_tambahan / panjang_cm / lebar_cm","        ▼","Services/PricingCalculator.cs          (satu-satunya rumus + validasi)","        │","        ▼","PricingResult  →  Pesanan_Detail (snapshot)  +  Pesanan.total_harga","```","","- **Backend adalah sumber kebenaran.** Client tidak pernah mengirim harga.","- **Satu jalur perhitungan** (`PricingCalculator`), dipakai untuk validasi","  (controller) dan untuk menyimpan pesanan (dalam transaksi DB).","","## 4. Pricing Mode yang Didukung","","`PricingMode` (`Models/Pricing.cs`):","","| Mode | Arti | Input dimensi | Harga |","|---|---|---|---|","| `Fixed` | harga tetap per unit | tidak ada | `unitPrice x qty` |","| `PerArea` | harga per m² | `width`, `height` | `width x height x rate x qty` |","| `PerLength` | harga per meter lurus | `length` | `length x rate x qty` |","| `PerUnit` | harga per unit/kuantitas | tidak ada | `unitPrice x qty` |","| `Custom` | cadangan | mengikuti aturan khusus | saat ini seperti `Fixed` |","","`Custom` disediakan agar mode baru bisa ditambahkan tanpa mengubah arsitektur","(lihat [§18](#18-cara-menambah-pricing-mode-baru)).","","## 5. Data Model","","### `product`","","| Kolom | Tipe | Keterangan |","|---|---|---|","| `harga` | `DECIMAL(15,2)` | Arti tergantung mode: harga satuan / per m² / per meter |","| `pricing_mode` | `VARCHAR(20)` NOT NULL DEFAULT `'Fixed'` | Mode harga |","| `dimension_unit` | `VARCHAR(20)` NOT NULL DEFAULT `'meter'` | Satuan input dimensi (`centimeter` / `meter`) |","","### `Ukuran_Produk` (varian)","","| Kolom | Tipe | Keterangan |","|---|---|---|","| `nama` | `VARCHAR(50)` | Nama ukuran, mis. `60 x 160 cm` |","| `harga_tambahan` | `DECIMAL(15,2)` | Tambahan harga (perilaku lama) |","| `harga` | `DECIMAL(15,2)` NULL | Harga absolut varian (override) |","| `panjang_cm` | `INT` NULL | Dimensi tetap varian (cm) |","| `lebar_cm` | `INT` NULL | Dimensi tetap varian (cm) |","","### `Pesanan_Detail` (snapshot)","","| Kolom | Tipe | Keterangan |","|---|---|---|","| `harga_satuan` | `DECIMAL(15,2)` | Rate snapshot (per unit / m² / meter) |","| `pricing_mode` | `VARCHAR(20)` NULL | Mode saat pesanan dibuat |","| `width_m` / `height_m` / `length_m` | `DECIMAL(12,4)` NULL | Dimensi ternormalisasi (meter) |","| `dimension_unit` | `VARCHAR(20)` NULL | Satuan input asli |","| `subtotal` | `DECIMAL(15,2)` NULL | Subtotal snapshot baris |","","`Pesanan.total_harga` = jumlah `subtotal` seluruh detail.","","## 6. Product vs Product Variant","","Varian product direpresentasikan oleh tabel **`Ukuran_Produk`** (sudah ada","sebelum perubahan ini). Harga dikonfigurasi di **kedua level** dengan aturan","sederhana dan tunggal:","","```","rate = varian.harga                       (jika diisi)","       ?? product.harga + varian.harga_tambahan","```","","Alasan:","","- **`Fixed` + ukuran tetap** (Standing Banner 60×160 vs 80×180) butuh harga","  per varian → pakai `Ukuran_Produk.harga` (`panjang_cm`/`lebar_cm` sebagai","  dimensi tetap).","- **`PerArea` / `PerLength` / `PerUnit`** pada dasarnya adalah rate di level","  product → cukup `product.harga`.","- Varian **tanpa** `Ukuran_Produk.harga` tetap memakai `harga_tambahan`,","  sehingga data lama tidak perlu diubah.","","`pricing_mode` **tidak** disimpan per varian; mode dimiliki product. Bila suatu","saat mode perlu berbeda per varian, tambahkan kolom","`Ukuran_Produk.pricing_mode` dan gabungkan di `PricingServices` — kalkulator","tidak perlu berubah.","","## 7. Rumus Harga","","```","rate = Ukuran_Produk.harga ?? (product.harga + Ukuran_Produk.harga_tambahan)","","Fixed / Custom :  subtotal = rate x qty","PerUnit        :  subtotal = rate x qty","PerArea        :  area     = width_m x height_m","                  subtotal = area x rate x qty","PerLength      :  subtotal = length_m x rate x qty","```","","Contoh hasil (sesuai tes otomatis):","","| Mode | Konfigurasi | Input | Subtotal |","|---|---|---|---|","| Fixed | rate 150.000 | qty 2 | **Rp300.000** |","| PerArea | rate 25.000/m² | 3 m × 1 m, qty 2 | **Rp150.000** |","| PerLength | rate 20.000/m | 5 m, qty 2 | **Rp200.000** |","| PerUnit | rate 50.000 | qty 3 | **Rp150.000** |","","Uang dibulatkan **2 desimal** dengan `MidpointRounding.AwayFromZero`","(`PricingCalculator.Round`), konsisten dengan `DECIMAL(15,2)`.","","## 8. Penanganan Satuan (Unit Handling)","","- **Satuan internal = METER.** Semua perhitungan m² / panjang memakai meter.","- `product.dimension_unit` menentukan satuan angka yang dikirim customer:","  `centimeter` atau `meter`.","- Konversi terpusat di `PricingUnits.ToMeters(value, unit)` —","  **tidak** disebar ke controller/service lain.","- Dimensi varian (`panjang_cm`, `lebar_cm`) selalu dalam cm (informatif; tidak","  dipakai sebagai faktor harga karena harga `Fixed` sudah per unit).","","Alur eksplisit:","","```","Input customer (satuan product.dimension_unit)","    ↓ validasi (\u003e 0)","    ↓ PricingUnits.ToMeters(...)","Nilai ternormalisasi (meter)","    ↓ PricingCalculator","Subtotal","```","","## 9. Aturan Validasi","","Validasi bergantung pada mode dan berada di `PricingCalculator` (dipakai ulang","oleh controller untuk pesan `400` yang jelas, lalu diulang di dalam transaksi","saat menyimpan pesanan):","","| Mode | Aturan |","|---|---|","| `Fixed` | `qty \u003e 0`; **menolak** `width`/`height`/`length` |","| `PerArea` | `width \u003e 0`, `height \u003e 0`, `qty \u003e 0`; menolak `length` |","| `PerLength` | `length \u003e 0`, `qty \u003e 0`; menolak `width`/`height` |","| `PerUnit` | `qty \u003e 0`; menolak `width`/`height`/`length` |","| `Custom` | mengikuti aturan khusus (saat ini seperti `Fixed`) |","","Dimensi yang tidak relevan **tidak** diabaikan diam-diam: mengirim","`width` pada product `Fixed` menghasilkan `400`.","","## 10. Perilaku API","","### Katalog product","","`GET /api/v1/products` menyertakan `pricingMode` dan `dimensionUnit`:","","```json","{","  \"id\": 1,","  \"nama\": \"Spanduk\",","  \"harga\": 25000.00,","  \"pricingMode\": \"PerArea\",","  \"dimensionUnit\": \"meter\"","}","```","","`POST /api/v1/products` (multipart) menerima form field opsional","`PricingMode` dan `DimensionUnit`. Nilai tak dikenal → `400`.","","`GET /api/v1/products/{id}/ukuran` mengembalikan daftar varian:","","```json","[","  {","    \"id\": 3,","    \"idProduct\": 1,","    \"nama\": \"60 x 160 cm\",","    \"hargaTambahan\": 0.00,","    \"harga\": 150000.00,","    \"panjangCm\": 60,","    \"lebarCm\": 160","  }","]","```","","### Buat / ubah pesanan","","`POST`/`PUT /api/v1/pesanan` tetap **`multipart/form-data`**. Field baru per","item (opsional, dipakai sesuai mode):","","| Form field | Tipe | Dipakai oleh |","|---|---|---|","| `items[N].width` | decimal | `PerArea` |","| `items[N].height` | decimal | `PerArea` |","| `items[N].length` | decimal | `PerLength` |","","`items[N].qty` selalu wajib. Satuan `width`/`height`/`length` mengikuti","`product.dimensionUnit`. **Tidak ada** field harga yang diterima; bila client","mengirim `price`/`harga`, field itu diabaikan total.","","Contoh `PerArea`:","","```","idAlamat: 5","items[0].idProduct: 1","items[0].width: 3","items[0].height: 1","items[0].qty: 2","```","","Contoh `Fixed` (Standing Banner, varian 60×160):","","```","idAlamat: 5","items[0].idProduct: 7","items[0].idUkuranProduk: 3","items[0].qty: 2","```","","`PesananDetailResponse` menambahkan `pricingMode`, `widthMeters`,","`heightMeters`, `lengthMeters`, `dimensionUnit`, `areaM2`, dan `subtotal`.","","## 11. Perilaku Cart","","Repo ini **tidak memiliki cart/keranjang di server**. Item \"cart\" hidup di","client dan dikirim langsung sebagai `items[]` pada `POST /api/v1/pesanan`.","Karena itu tidak ada tabel cart baru.","","Data minimum per item yang harus disimpan/dikirim client:","","- `idProduct` (wajib)","- `idUkuranProduk` (opsional, untuk varian)","- `qty` (wajib)","- `width` / `height` / `length` (sesuai mode)","","`subtotal`/`area` **tidak** disimpan di cart karena selalu bisa dihitung ulang.","Client boleh menghitung estimasi untuk UI, tetapi server selalu menghitung","ulang harga final.","","## 12. Perilaku Order","","`PesananServices.CreatePesananAsync` / `UpdatePesananAsync`:","","1. membuka transaksi,","2. untuk setiap item memanggil `PricingServices.EvaluateAsync(conn, tx, input)`,","3. bila product/varian tidak valid atau validasi gagal → rollback, `400`,","4. menjumlahkan `PricingResult.Subtotal` ke `Pesanan.total_harga`,","5. menulis snapshot ke `Pesanan_Detail`.","","`UpdatePesananAsync` mengganti seluruh detail (detail lama di-soft delete),","tetap menghitung ulang harga dari konfigurasi saat itu.","","## 13. Preservasi Harga Historis","","Harga pesanan lama tidak boleh berubah bila harga product diubah:","","- `Pesanan_Detail.harga_satuan` menyimpan **rate** snapshot.","- `Pesanan_Detail.subtotal` menyimpan **subtotal** snapshot (bukan dihitung","  ulang dari `product.harga` saat dibaca).","- `width_m`/`height_m`/`length_m` dan `pricing_mode` menyimpan konteks","  perhitungan.","- `Pesanan.total_harga` adalah snapshot total.","","Read query memakai `COALESCE(d.subtotal, d.harga_satuan * d.qty)` sehingga baris","lama (sebelum migrasi) tetap benar. `areaM2` dihitung ulang saat query","(`width_m * height_m`) karena tidak perlu disimpan.","","## 14. Migrasi Database","","Fresh install: [`Schema/setup.sql`](../Schema/setup.sql) sudah memuat kolom baru.","","Database berjalan:","","```bash","mysql -u \u003cuser\u003e -p katalog_percetakan \u003c Schema/migration_pricing_modes.sql","```","","Migrasi bersifat **idempotent** (dicek lewat `information_schema`) dan **tidak","destruktif**:","","- Menambah 2 kolom `product`, 3 kolom `Ukuran_Produk`, 6 kolom","  `Pesanan_Detail` (semua dengan `DEFAULT` / `NULL`).","- Backfill aman:","  - `Pesanan_Detail.pricing_mode = 'Fixed'` bila NULL,","  - `Pesanan_Detail.dimension_unit = 'meter'` bila NULL,","  - `Pesanan_Detail.subtotal = harga_satuan * qty` bila NULL.","","Tidak ada kolom lama yang dihapus.","","## 15. Kompatibilitas Mundur (Backward Compatibility)","","- **Product lama** otomatis `pricing_mode = 'Fixed'` dan","  `dimension_unit = 'meter'`; rumusnya identik dengan sebelumnya","  (`harga + harga_tambahan`) × qty.","- **Varian lama** (`Ukuran_Produk.harga` NULL) tetap memakai `harga_tambahan`.","- **Klien lama** yang hanya mengirim `idProduct`, `idUkuranProduk`, `qty`","  tetap bekerja untuk product `Fixed`.","- **Pesanan lama** tetap terbaca; `subtotal` di-backfill.","- Product yang ingin memakai `PerArea`/`PerLength`/`PerUnit` **harus**","  dikonfigurasi manual (lihat [§17](#17-cara-menambah-product-baru)) — tidak","  ditebak otomatis karena salah tebak berarti salah harga.","","## 16. Testing","","Test ada di `tests/Percetakan.Tests/` (xUnit) dan menguji","`PricingCalculator` secara murni (tanpa database):","","| # | Test | Cakupan |","|---|---|---|","| 1 | `Fixed_UsesVariantPrice_TimesQuantity` | Fixed Rp300.000 |","| 2 | `Fixed_LegacyProduct_UsesBasePlusAdditional` | kompatibilitas lama |","| 3 | `PerArea_MultipliesAreaRateQuantity` | PerArea Rp150.000 |","| 4 | `PerArea_ConvertsCentimeterInputToMeters` | konversi cm → m |","| 5 | `PerLength_MultipliesLengthRateQuantity` | PerLength Rp200.000 |","| 6 | `PerUnit_MultipliesUnitPriceQuantity` | PerUnit Rp150.000 |","| 7 | `PerArea_RejectsZeroWidth` | validasi width = 0 |","| 8 | `Quantity_Zero_FailsForEveryMode` | qty = 0 gagal (semua mode) |","| 9 | `PricingInput_ExposesNoClientPriceField` | client tidak bisa set harga |","| 10 | `LegacyProduct_DefaultsToFixedMode` | product lama → Fixed |","| 11 | `PerArea_RoundsMoneyToTwoDecimals` | pembulatan uang |","| 12 | `PricingUnitsTests` | parsing \u0026 konversi satuan/mode |","","Jalankan:","","```bash","dotnet test tests/Percetakan.Tests/Percetakan.Tests.csproj","```","","## 17. Cara Menambah Product Baru","","Menambah product **normalnya operasi data**, bukan perubahan kode:","","```","Product Baru","    ↓","Pilih PricingMode        (Fixed / PerArea / PerLength / PerUnit)","    ↓","Set product.harga        (harga satuan / per m² / per meter)","    ↓","Set product.dimension_unit (meter / centimeter; hanya untuk PerArea/PerLength)","    ↓","(Perlu varian? Isi Ukuran_Produk: nama, harga, panjang_cm, lebar_cm)","    ↓","Validasi \u0026 simpan","```","","### Contoh: Banner (per m²)","","```sql","INSERT INTO product","    (idKategoriProduct, idStatusProduct, nama, deskripsi, harga,","     pricing_mode, dimension_unit)","VALUES","    (2, 2, 'Spanduk', 'Cetak spanduk bahan flexi', 25000,","     'PerArea', 'meter');","```","","Customer mengirim `width`, `height`, `qty`.","","### Contoh: Standing Banner (fixed, dua varian)","","```sql","INSERT INTO product","    (idKategoriProduct, idStatusProduct, nama, deskripsi, harga,","     pricing_mode, dimension_unit)","VALUES","    (2, 2, 'Standing Banner', 'Banner berdiri', 0,","     'Fixed', 'meter');","","INSERT INTO Ukuran_Produk (idProduct, nama, harga, panjang_cm, lebar_cm)","VALUES","    (LAST_INSERT_ID(), '60 x 160 cm', 150000, 60, 160),","    (LAST_INSERT_ID(), '80 x 180 cm', 200000, 80, 180);","```","","Customer hanya mengirim `idProduct`, `idUkuranProduk`, `qty`.","","### Contoh: Business Card (per unit)","","```sql","INSERT INTO product","    (idKategoriProduct, idStatusProduct, nama, deskripsi, harga,","     pricing_mode, dimension_unit)","VALUES","    (2, 2, 'Kartu Nama', 'Cetak kartu nama', 50000,","     'PerUnit', 'meter');","```","","### Contoh: Kain (per meter lurus)","","```sql","INSERT INTO product","    (idKategoriProduct, idStatusProduct, nama, deskripsi, harga,","     pricing_mode, dimension_unit)","VALUES","    (2, 2, 'Kain Custom', 'Cetak kain per meter', 20000,","     'PerLength', 'meter');","```","","Customer mengirim `length`, `qty`.","","## 18. Cara Menambah Pricing Mode Baru","","Mode baru ditambahkan **tanpa menyentuh** controller, order, atau schema:","","1. Tambahkan nilai pada `enum PricingMode` (`Models/Pricing.cs`).","2. Tambahkan method kalkulasi di `PricingCalculator.cs` dan cabang pada","   `Calculate` (mis. `PricingMode.PerVolume =\u003e CalculatePerVolume(...)`).","3. Bila butuh input baru, tambahkan field di `PricingInput` dan (bila perlu","   dikirim client) pada `PesananDetailRequest` + parsing multipart.","4. Tambahkan test di `tests/Percetakan.Tests/`.","5. **Tidak perlu migrasi** untuk kolom mode (tersimpan sebagai string).","   Tambah kolom hanya bila perlu menyimpan data baru.","","Contoh kerangka:","","```csharp","private static PricingResult CalculatePerVolume(","    PricingConfiguration config,","    PricingInput input) { /* rumus baru */ }","```","","## 19. Diagram Arsitektur","","```mermaid","flowchart TD","    P[Product\u003cbr/\u003epricing_mode, harga, dimension_unit]","    V[Ukuran_Produk / ProductVariant\u003cbr/\u003eharga, harga_tambahan, panjang_cm, lebar_cm]","    C[CustomerInput\u003cbr/\u003ewidth / height / length / qty]","    PS[PricingServices\u003cbr/\u003emuat konfigurasi dari DB]","    PC[PricingCalculator\u003cbr/\u003evalidasi + rumus]","    R[PricingResult\u003cbr/\u003erate, dimensi, area, subtotal]","    D[Pesanan_Detail\u003cbr/\u003esnapshot harga]","    O[Pesanan.total_harga]","","    P --\u003e PS","    V --\u003e PS","    C --\u003e PC","    PS --\u003e PC","    PC --\u003e R","    R --\u003e D","    R --\u003e O","```","","Alur cart di frontend (tidak ada cart server): `CustomerInput` dirakit client,","dikirim ke `POST /api/v1/pesanan`, lalu server menghitung ulang lewat","`PricingServices → containertiming="hpc"><article class="markdown-body entry-content container-lg" itemprop="text"><div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Pricing System (Sistem Harga)</h1><a id="user-content-pricing-system-sistem-harga" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Pricing System (Sistem Harga)" href="#pricing-system-sistem-harga"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Dokumen ini menjelaskan bagaimana harga product dihitung di Katalog
Percetakan. Sebelumnya aplikasi hanya punya <strong>satu</strong> cara menghitung harga:
<code>harga product + harga tambahan varian</code>, dikali <code>qty</code>. Sekarang setiap product
<strong>mendefinisikan sendiri</strong> cara harganya dihitung lewat konfigurasi — bukan
lewat nama product.</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Model/domain: <a href="/MhdFarhannn/Katalog-Percetakan/blob/main/Models/Pricing.cs"><code>Models/Pricing.cs</code></a></li>
<li>Kalkulasi (sumber kebenaran): <a href="/MhdFarhannn/Katalog-Percetakan/blob/main/Services/PricingCalculator.cs"><code>Services/PricingCalculator.cs</code></a></li>
<li>Pembacaan konfigurasi dari DB: <a href="/MhdFarhannn/Katalog-Percetakan/blob/main/Services/PricingServices.cs"><code>Services/PricingServices.cs</code></a></li>
<li>Migrasi database: <a href="/MhdFarhannn/Katalog-Percetakan/blob/main/Schema/migration_pricing_modes.sql"><code>Schema/migration_pricing_modes.sql</code></a></li>
<li>Tes: <a href="/MhdFarhannn/Katalog-Percetakan/blob/main/tests/Percetakan.Tests/PricingCalculatorTests.cs"><code>tests/Percetakan.Tests/PricingCalculatorTests.cs</code></a></li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">1. Mengapa Banyak Pricing Mode Dibutuhkan</h2><a id="user-content-1-mengapa-banyak-pricing-mode-dibutuhkan" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 1. Mengapa Banyak Pricing Mode Dibutuhkan" href="#1-mengapa-banyak-pricing-mode-dibutuhkan"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Product percetakan tidak semuanya dihargai dengan cara yang sama:</p>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Product</th>
<th>Cara harga wajar</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td>Spanduk / Banner</td>
<td>per luas (m²), karena ukuran bebas</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Standing Banner</td>
<td>harga tetap per unit, ukuran sudah ditentukan</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Business Card</td>
<td>per unit / kuantitas</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Sticker</td>
<td>bisa per luas</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Kain / Fabric</td>
<td>bisa per meter lurus</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Brosur</td>
<td>per kuantitas</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<p dir="auto">Memaksa semua product memakai satu rumus (mis. per meter) membuat product
fixed-size salah harga, dan menebak perilaku dari <code>product.nama</code> rapuh:
mengganti nama product akan mengubah harga. Karena itu perilaku harga
dijadikan <strong>konfigurasi per product</strong>.</p>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Aturan penting:</strong> jangan pernah menulis <code>if (product.Nama == "Banner")</code>.
Perilaku harga hanya boleh berasal dari <code>product.pricing_mode</code>.</p>
</blockquote>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">2. Arsitektur Harga Sebelumnya</h2><a id="user-content-2-arsitektur-harga-sebelumnya" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 2. Arsitektur Harga Sebelumnya" href="#2-arsitektur-harga-sebelumnya"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="product.harga  +  Ukuran_Produk.harga_tambahan   (jika ukuran dipilih)
        │
        ▼
   harga_satuan  x  qty  =  Pesanan_Detail / Pesanan.total_harga"><pre class="notranslate"><code>product.harga  +  Ukuran_Produk.harga_tambahan   (jika ukuran dipilih)
        │
        ▼
   harga_satuan  x  qty  =  Pesanan_Detail / Pesanan.total_harga
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Semua logika ada di <code>Services/PesananServices.cs</code> (<code>GetHargaSatuanAsync</code>),
tidak ada validasi dimensi, dan tidak ada cara menyatakan "harga per m²".</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">3. Arsitektur Harga Sekarang</h2><a id="user-content-3-arsitektur-harga-sekarang" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 3. Arsitektur Harga Sekarang" href="#3-arsitektur-harga-sekarang"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="Customer input (width/height/length/qty)
        │
        ▼
Controller/PesananController.cs        (parsing + validasi awal)
        │
        ▼
Services/PricingServices.cs            (baca konfigurasi dari DB)
        │  product.pricing_mode / harga / dimension_unit
        │  Ukuran_Produk.harga / harga_tambahan / panjang_cm / lebar_cm
        ▼
Services/PricingCalculator.cs          (satu-satunya rumus + validasi)
        │
        ▼
PricingResult  →  Pesanan_Detail (snapshot)  +  Pesanan.total_harga"><pre class="notranslate"><code>Customer input (width/height/length/qty)
        │
        ▼
Controller/PesananController.cs        (parsing + validasi awal)
        │
        ▼
Services/PricingServices.cs            (baca konfigurasi dari DB)
        │  product.pricing_mode / harga / dimension_unit
        │  Ukuran_Produk.harga / harga_tambahan / panjang_cm / lebar_cm
        ▼
Services/PricingCalculator.cs          (satu-satunya rumus + validasi)
        │
        ▼
PricingResult  →  Pesanan_Detail (snapshot)  +  Pesanan.total_harga
</code></pre></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Backend adalah sumber kebenaran.</strong> Client tidak pernah mengirim harga.</li>
<li><strong>Satu jalur perhitungan</strong> (<code>PricingCalculator</code>), dipakai untuk validasi
(controller) dan untuk menyimpan pesanan (dalam transaksi DB).</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">4. Pricing Mode yang Didukung</h2><a id="user-content-4-pricing-mode-yang-didukung" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 4. Pricing Mode yang Didukung" href="#4-pricing-mode-yang-didukung"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><code>PricingMode</code> (<code>Models/Pricing.cs</code>):</p>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Mode</th>
<th>Arti</th>
<th>Input dimensi</th>
<th>Harga</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>Fixed</code></td>
<td>harga tetap per unit</td>
<td>tidak ada</td>
<td><code>unitPrice x qty</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>PerArea</code></td>
<td>harga per m²</td>
<td><code>width</code>, <code>height</code></td>
<td><code>width x height x rate x qty</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>PerLength</code></td>
<td>harga per meter lurus</td>
<td><code>length</code></td>
<td><code>length x rate x qty</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>PerUnit</code></td>
<td>harga per unit/kuantitas</td>
<td>tidak ada</td>
<td><code>unitPrice x qty</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>Custom</code></td>
<td>cadangan</td>
<td>mengikuti aturan khusus</td>
<td>saat ini seperti <code>Fixed</code></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<p dir="auto"><code>Custom</code> disediakan agar mode baru bisa ditambahkan tanpa mengubah arsitektur
(lihat <a href="#18-cara-menambah-pricing-mode-baru">§18</a>).</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">5. Data Model</h2><a id="user-content-5-data-model" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 5. Data Model" href="#5-data-model"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto"><code>Ukuran_Produk</code> (varian)</h3><a id="user-content-ukuran_produk-varian" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Ukuran_Produk (varian)" href="#ukuran_produk-varian"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
