<p dir="auto"><code>Pesanan.total_harga</code> = jumlah <code>subtotal</code> seluruh detail.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">6. Product vs Product Variant</h2><a id="user-content-6-product-vs-product-variant" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 6. Product vs Product Variant" href="#6-product-vs-product-variant"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Varian product direpresentasikan oleh tabel <strong><code>Ukuran_Produk</code></strong> (sudah ada
sebelum perubahan ini). Harga dikonfigurasi di <strong>kedua level</strong> dengan aturan
sederhana dan tunggal:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="rate = varian.harga                       (jika diisi)
       ?? product.harga + varian.harga_tambahan"><pre class="notranslate"><code>rate = varian.harga                       (jika diisi)
       ?? product.harga + varian.harga_tambahan
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Alasan:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong><code>Fixed</code> + ukuran tetap</strong> (Standing Banner 60×160 vs 80×180) butuh harga
per varian → pakai <code>Ukuran_Produk.harga</code> (<code>panjang_cm</code>/<code>lebar_cm</code> sebagai
dimensi tetap).</li>
<li><strong><code>PerArea</code> / <code>PerLength</code> / <code>PerUnit</code></strong> pada dasarnya adalah rate di level
product → cukup <code>product.harga</code>.</li>
<li>Varian <strong>tanpa</strong> <code>Ukuran_Produk.harga</code> tetap memakai <code>harga_tambahan</code>,
sehingga data lama tidak perlu diubah.</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><code>pricing_mode</code> <strong>tidak</strong> disimpan per varian; mode dimiliki product. Bila suatu
saat mode perlu berbeda per varian, tambahkan kolom
<code>Ukuran_Produk.pricing_mode</code> dan gabungkan di <code>PricingServices</code> — kalkulator
tidak perlu berubah.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">7. Rumus Harga</h2><a id="user-content-7-rumus-harga" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 7. Rumus Harga" href="#7-rumus-harga"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="rate = Ukuran_Produk.harga ?? (product.harga + Ukuran_Produk.harga_tambahan)

Fixed / Custom :  subtotal = rate x qty
PerUnit        :  subtotal = rate x qty
PerArea        :  area     = width_m x height_m
                  subtotal = area x rate x qty
PerLength      :  subtotal = length_m x rate x qty"><pre class="notranslate"><code>rate = Ukuran_Produk.harga ?? (product.harga + Ukuran_Produk.harga_tambahan)

Fixed / Custom :  subtotal = rate x qty
PerUnit        :  subtotal = rate x qty
PerArea        :  area     = width_m x height_m
                  subtotal = area x rate x qty
PerLength      :  subtotal = length_m x rate x qty
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Contoh hasil (sesuai tes otomatis):</p>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Mode</th>
<th>Konfigurasi</th>
<th>Input</th>
<th>Subtotal</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td>Fixed</td>
<td>rate 150.000</td>
<td>qty 2</td>
<td><strong>Rp300.000</strong></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>PerArea</td>
<td>rate 25.000/m²</td>
<td>3 m × 1 m, qty 2</td>
<td><strong>Rp150.000</strong></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>PerLength</td>
<td>rate 20.000/m</td>
<td>5 m, qty 2</td>
<td><strong>Rp200.000</strong></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>PerUnit</td>
<td>rate 50.000</td>
<td>qty 3</td>
<td><strong>Rp150.000</strong></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<p dir="auto">Uang dibulatkan <strong>2 desimal</strong> dengan <code>MidpointRounding.AwayFromZero</code>
(<code>PricingCalculator.Round</code>), konsisten dengan <code>DECIMAL(15,2)</code>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">8. Penanganan Satuan (Unit Handling)</h2><a id="user-content-8-penanganan-satuan-unit-handling" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 8. Penanganan Satuan (Unit Handling)" href="#8-penanganan-satuan-unit-handling"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Satuan internal = METER.</strong> Semua perhitungan m² / panjang memakai meter.</li>
<li><code>product.dimension_unit</code> menentukan satuan angka yang dikirim customer:
<code>centimeter</code> atau <code>meter</code>.</li>
<li>Konversi terpusat di <code>PricingUnits.ToMeters(value, unit)</code> —
<strong>tidak</strong> disebar ke controller/service lain.</li>
<li>Dimensi varian (<code>panjang_cm</code>, <code>lebar_cm</code>) selalu dalam cm (informatif; tidak
dipakai sebagai faktor harga karena harga <code>Fixed</code> sudah per unit).</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Alur eksplisit:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="Input customer (satuan product.dimension_unit)
    ↓ validasi (&gt; 0)
    ↓ PricingUnits.ToMeters(...)
Nilai ternormalisasi (meter)
    ↓ PricingCalculator
Subtotal"><pre class="notranslate"><code>Input customer (satuan product.dimension_unit)
    ↓ validasi (&gt; 0)
    ↓ PricingUnits.ToMeters(...)
Nilai ternormalisasi (meter)
    ↓ PricingCalculator
Subtotal
</code></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">9. Aturan Validasi</h2><a id="user-content-9-aturan-validasi" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 9. Aturan Validasi" href="#9-aturan-validasi"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Validasi bergantung pada mode dan berada di <code>PricingCalculator</code> (dipakai ulang
oleh controller untuk pesan <code>400</code> yang jelas, lalu diulang di dalam transaksi
saat menyimpan pesanan):</p>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Mode</th>
<th>Aturan</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>Fixed</code></td>
<td><code>qty &gt; 0</code>; <strong>menolak</strong> <code>width</code>/<code>height</code>/<code>length</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>PerArea</code></td>
<td><code>width &gt; 0</code>, <code>height &gt; 0</code>, <code>qty &gt; 0</code>; menolak <code>length</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>PerLength</code></td>
<td><code>length &gt; 0</code>, <code>qty &gt; 0</code>; menolak <code>width</code>/<code>height</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>PerUnit</code></td>
<td><code>qty &gt; 0</code>; menolak <code>width</code>/<code>height</code>/<code>length</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>Custom</code></td>
<td>mengikuti aturan khusus (saat ini seperti <code>Fixed</code>)</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<p dir="auto">Dimensi yang tidak relevan <strong>tidak</strong> diabaikan diam-diam: mengirim
<code>width</code> pada product <code>Fixed</code> menghasilkan <code>400</code>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">10. Perilaku API</h2><a id="user-content-10-perilaku-api" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 10. Perilaku API" href="#10-perilaku-api"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Katalog product</h3><a id="user-content-katalog-product" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Katalog product" href="#katalog-product"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><code>GET /api/v1/products</code> menyertakan <code>pricingMode</code> dan <code>dimensionUnit</code>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-json notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="{
  &quot;id&quot;: 1,
  &quot;nama&quot;: &quot;Spanduk&quot;,
  &quot;harga&quot;: 25000.00,
  &quot;pricingMode&quot;: &quot;PerArea&quot;,
  &quot;dimensionUnit&quot;: &quot;meter&quot;
}"><pre>{
  <span class="pl-ent">"id"</span>: <span class="pl-c1">1</span>,
  <span class="pl-ent">"nama"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>Spanduk<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>,
  <span class="pl-ent">"harga"</span>: <span class="pl-c1">25000.00</span>,
  <span class="pl-ent">"pricingMode"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>PerArea<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>,
  <span class="pl-ent">"dimensionUnit"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>meter<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
}</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><code>POST /api/v1/products</code> (multipart) menerima form field opsional
<code>PricingMode</code> dan <code>DimensionUnit</code>. Nilai tak dikenal → <code>400</code>.</p>
<p dir="auto"><code>GET /api/v1/products/{id}/ukuran</code> mengembalikan daftar varian:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-json notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="[
  {
    &quot;id&quot;: 3,
    &quot;idProduct&quot;: 1,
    &quot;nama&quot;: &quot;60 x 160 cm&quot;,
    &quot;hargaTambahan&quot;: 0.00,
    &quot;harga&quot;: 150000.00,
    &quot;panjangCm&quot;: 60,
    &quot;lebarCm&quot;: 160
  }
]"><pre>[
  {
    <span class="pl-ent">"id"</span>: <span class="pl-c1">3</span>,
    <span class="pl-ent">"idProduct"</span>: <span class="pl-c1">1</span>,
    <span class="pl-ent">"nama"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>60 x 160 cm<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>,
    <span class="pl-ent">"hargaTambahan"</span>: <span class="pl-c1">0.00</span>,
    <span class="pl-ent">"harga"</span>: <span class="pl-c1">150000.00</span>,
    <span class="pl-ent">"panjangCm"</span>: <span class="pl-c1">60</span>,
    <span class="pl-ent">"lebarCm"</span>: <span class="pl-c1">160</span>
  }
]</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Buat / ubah pesanan</h3><a id="user-content-buat--ubah-pesanan" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Buat / ubah pesanan" href="#buat--ubah-pesanan"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><code>POST</code>/<code>PUT /api/v1/pesanan</code> tetap <strong><code>multipart/form-data</code></strong>. Field baru per
item (opsional, dipakai sesuai mode):</p>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Form field</th>
<th>Tipe</th>
<th>Dipakai oleh</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>items[N].width</code></td>
<td>decimal</td>
<td><code>PerArea</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>items[N].height</code></td>
<td>decimal</td>
<td><code>PerArea</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>items[N].length</code></td>
<td>decimal</td>
<td><code>PerLength</code></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<p dir="auto"><code>items[N].qty</code> selalu wajib. Satuan <code>width</code>/<code>height</code>/<code>length</code> mengikuti
<code>product.dimensionUnit</code>. <strong>Tidak ada</strong> field harga yang diterima; bila client
mengirim <code>price</code>/<code>harga</code>, field itu diabaikan total.</p>
<p dir="auto">Contoh <code>PerArea</code>:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="idAlamat: 5
items[0].idProduct: 1
items[0].width: 3
items[0].height: 1
items[0].qty: 2"><pre class="notranslate"><code>idAlamat: 5
items[0].idProduct: 1
items[0].width: 3
items[0].height: 1
items[0].qty: 2
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Contoh <code>Fixed</code> (Standing Banner, varian 60×160):</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="idAlamat: 5
items[0].idProduct: 7
items[0].idUkuranProduk: 3
items[0].qty: 2"><pre class="notranslate"><code>idAlamat: 5
items[0].idProduct: 7
items[0].idUkuranProduk: 3
items[0].qty: 2
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><code>PesananDetailResponse</code> menambahkan <code>pricingMode</code>, <code>widthMeters</code>,
<code>heightMeters</code>, <code>lengthMeters</code>, <code>dimensionUnit</code>, <code>areaM2</code>, dan <code>subtotal</code>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">11. Perilaku Cart</h2><a id="user-content-11-perilaku-cart" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 11. Perilaku Cart" href="#11-perilaku-cart"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Repo ini <strong>tidak memiliki cart/keranjang di server</strong>. Item "cart" hidup di
client dan dikirim langsung sebagai <code>items[]</code> pada <code>POST /api/v1/pesanan</code>.
Karena itu tidak ada tabel cart baru.</p>
<p dir="auto">Data minimum per item yang harus disimpan/dikirim client:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>idProduct</code> (wajib)</li>
<li><code>idUkuranProduk</code> (opsional, untuk varian)</li>
<li><code>qty</code> (wajib)</li>
<li><code>width</code> / <code>height</code> / <code>length</code> (sesuai mode)</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><code>subtotal</code>/<code>area</code> <strong>tidak</strong> disimpan di cart karena selalu bisa dihitung ulang.
Client boleh menghitung estimasi untuk UI, tetapi server selalu menghitung
ulang harga final.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">12. Perilaku Order</h2><a id="user-content-12-perilaku-order" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 12. Perilaku Order" href="#12-perilaku-order"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><code>PesananServices.CreatePesananAsync</code> / <code>UpdatePesananAsync</code>:</p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>membuka transaksi,</li>
<li>untuk setiap item memanggil <code>PricingServices.EvaluateAsync(conn, tx, input)</code>,</li>
<li>bila product/varian tidak valid atau validasi gagal → rollback, <code>400</code>,</li>
<li>menjumlahkan <code>PricingResult.Subtotal</code> ke <code>Pesanan.total_harga</code>,</li>
<li>menulis snapshot ke <code>Pesanan_Detail</code>.</li>
</ol>
<p dir="auto"><code>UpdatePesananAsync</code> mengganti seluruh detail (detail lama di-soft delete),
tetap menghitung ulang harga dari konfigurasi saat itu.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">13. Preservasi Harga Historis</h2><a id="user-content-13-preservasi-harga-historis" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 13. Preservasi Harga Historis" href="#13-preservasi-harga-historis"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Harga pesanan lama tidak boleh berubah bila harga product diubah:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>Pesanan_Detail.harga_satuan</code> menyimpan <strong>rate</strong> snapshot.</li>
<li><code>Pesanan_Detail.subtotal</code> menyimpan <strong>subtotal</strong> snapshot (bukan dihitung
ulang dari <code>product.harga</code> saat dibaca).</li>
<li><code>width_m</code>/<code>height_m</code>/<code>length_m</code> dan <code>pricing_mode</code> menyimpan konteks
perhitungan.</li>
<li><code>Pesanan.total_harga</code> adalah snapshot total.</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Read query memakai <code>COALESCE(d.subtotal, d.harga_satuan * d.qty)</code> sehingga baris
lama (sebelum migrasi) tetap benar. <code>areaM2</code> dihitung ulang saat query
(<code>width_m * height_m</code>) karena tidak perlu disimpan.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">14. Migrasi Database</h2><a id="user-content-14-migrasi-database" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 14. Migrasi Database" href="#14-migrasi-database"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Fresh install: <a href="/MhdFarhannn/Katalog-Percetakan/blob/main/Schema/setup.sql"><code>Schema/setup.sql</code></a> sudah memuat kolom baru.</p>
<p dir="auto">Database berjalan:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="mysql -u &lt;user&gt; -p katalog_percetakan &lt; Schema/migration_pricing_modes.sql"><pre>mysql -u <span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>user<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span> -p katalog_percetakan <span class="pl-k">&lt;</span> Schema/migration_pricing_modes.sql</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Migrasi bersifat <strong>idempotent</strong> (dicek lewat <code>information_schema</code>) dan <strong>tidak
destruktif</strong>:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Menambah 2 kolom <code>product</code>, 3 kolom <code>Ukuran_Produk</code>, 6 kolom
<code>Pesanan_Detail</code> (semua dengan <code>DEFAULT</code> / <code>NULL</code>).</li>
<li>Backfill aman:
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>Pesanan_Detail.pricing_mode = 'Fixed'</code> bila NULL,</li>
<li><code>Pesanan_Detail.dimension_unit = 'meter'</code> bila NULL,</li>
<li><code>Pesanan_Detail.subtotal = harga_satuan * qty</code> bila NULL.</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Tidak ada kolom lama yang dihapus.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">15. Kompatibilitas Mundur (Backward Compatibility)</h2><a id="user-content-15-kompatibilitas-mundur-backward-compatibility" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 15. Kompatibilitas Mundur (Backward Compatibility)" href="#15-kompatibilitas-mundur-backward-compatibility"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Product lama</strong> otomatis <code>pricing_mode = 'Fixed'</code> dan
<code>dimension_unit = 'meter'</code>; rumusnya identik dengan sebelumnya
(<code>harga + harga_tambahan</code>) × qty.</li>
<li><strong>Varian lama</strong> (<code>Ukuran_Produk.harga</code> NULL) tetap memakai <code>harga_tambahan</code>.</li>
<li><strong>Klien lama</strong> yang hanya mengirim <code>idProduct</code>, <code>idUkuranProduk</code>, <code>qty</code>
tetap bekerja untuk product <code>Fixed</code>.</li>
<li><strong>Pesanan lama</strong> tetap terbaca; <code>subtotal</code> di-backfill.</li>
<li>Product yang ingin memakai <code>PerArea</code>/<code>PerLength</code>/<code>PerUnit</code> <strong>harus</strong>
dikonfigurasi manual (lihat <a href="#17-cara-menambah-product-baru">§17</a>) — tidak
ditebak otomatis karena salah tebak berarti salah harga.</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">16. Testing</h2><a id="user-content-16-testing" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 16. Testing" href="#16-testing"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Test ada di <code>tests/Percetakan.Tests/</code> (xUnit) dan menguji
<code>PricingCalculator</code> secara murni (tanpa database):</p>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>#</th>
<th>Test</th>
<th>Cakupan</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td>1</td>
<td><code>Fixed_UsesVariantPrice_TimesQuantity</code></td>
<td>Fixed Rp300.000</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>2</td>
<td><code>Fixed_LegacyProduct_UsesBasePlusAdditional</code></td>
<td>kompatibilitas lama</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>3</td>
<td><code>PerArea_MultipliesAreaRateQuantity</code></td>
<td>PerArea Rp150.000</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>4</td>
<td><code>PerArea_ConvertsCentimeterInputToMeters</code></td>
<td>konversi cm → m</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>5</td>
<td><code>PerLength_MultipliesLengthRateQuantity</code></td>
<td>PerLength Rp200.000</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>6</td>
<td><code>PerUnit_MultipliesUnitPriceQuantity</code></td>
<td>PerUnit Rp150.000</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>7</td>
<td><code>PerArea_RejectsZeroWidth</code></td>
<td>validasi width = 0</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>8</td>
<td><code>Quantity_Zero_FailsForEveryMode</code></td>
<td>qty = 0 gagal (semua mode)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>9</td>
<td><code>PricingInput_ExposesNoClientPriceField</code></td>
<td>client tidak bisa set harga</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>10</td>
<td><code>LegacyProduct_DefaultsToFixedMode</code></td>
<td>product lama → Fixed</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>11</td>
<td><code>PerArea_RoundsMoneyToTwoDecimals</code></td>
<td>pembulatan uang</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>12</td>
<td><code>PricingUnitsTests</code></td>
<td>parsing &amp; konversi satuan/mode</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<p dir="auto">Jalankan:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="dotnet test tests/Percetakan.Tests/Percetakan.Tests.csproj"><pre>dotnet <span class="pl-c1">test</span> tests/Percetakan.Tests/Percetakan.Tests.csproj</pre></div>

Cara Menambah Product Baru</h2><a id="user-content-17-cara-menambah-product-baru" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 17. Cara Menambah Product Baru" href="#17-cara-menambah-product-baru"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Menambah product <strong>normalnya operasi data</strong>, bukan perubahan kode:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="Product Baru
    ↓
Pilih PricingMode        (Fixed / PerArea / PerLength / PerUnit)
    ↓
Set product.harga        (harga satuan / per m² / per meter)
    ↓
Set product.dimension_unit (meter / centimeter; hanya untuk PerArea/PerLength)
    ↓
(Perlu varian? Isi Ukuran_Produk: nama, harga, panjang_cm, lebar_cm)
    ↓
Validasi &amp; simpan"><pre class="notranslate"><code>Product Baru
    ↓
Pilih PricingMode        (Fixed / PerArea / PerLength / PerUnit)
    ↓
Set product.harga        (harga satuan / per m² / per meter)
    ↓
Set product.dimension_unit (meter / centimeter; hanya untuk PerArea/PerLength)
    ↓
(Perlu varian? Isi Ukuran_Produk: nama, harga, panjang_cm, lebar_cm)
    ↓
Validasi &amp; simpan
</code></pre></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">18. Cara Menambah Pricing Mode Baru</h2><a id="user-content-18-cara-menambah-pricing-mode-baru" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 18. Cara Menambah Pricing Mode Baru" href="#18-cara-menambah-pricing-mode-baru"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Mode baru ditambahkan <strong>tanpa menyentuh</strong> controller, order, atau schema:</p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>Tambahkan nilai pada <code>enum PricingMode</code> (<code>Models/Pricing.cs</code>).</li>
<li>Tambahkan method kalkulasi di <code>PricingCalculator.cs</code> dan cabang pada
<code>Calculate</code> (mis. <code>PricingMode.PerVolume =&gt; CalculatePerVolume(...)</code>).</li>
<li>Bila butuh input baru, tambahkan field di <code>PricingInput</code> dan (bila perlu
dikirim client) pada <code>PesananDetailRequest</code> + parsing multipart.</li>
<li>Tambahkan test di <code>tests/Percetakan.Tests/</code>.</li>
<li><strong>Tidak perlu migrasi</strong> untuk kolom mode (tersimpan sebagai string).
Tambah kolom hanya bila perlu menyimpan data baru.</li>
</ol>
<p dir="auto">Contoh kerangka:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-cs notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="private static PricingResult CalculatePerVolume(
    PricingConfiguration config,
    PricingInput input) { /* rumus baru */ }"><pre><span class="pl-k">private</span> <span class="pl-k"><span class="pl-k">static</span></span> <span class="pl-smi">PricingResult</span> <span class="pl-en">CalculatePerVolume</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span>
    <span class="pl-smi">PricingConfiguration</span> <span class="pl-s1">config</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
    <span class="pl-smi">PricingInput</span> <span class="pl-s1">input</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span> <span class="pl-c">/* rumus baru */</span> <span class="pl-kos">}</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">19. Diagram Arsitektur</h2><a id="user-content-19-diagram-arsitektur" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 19. Diagram Arsitektur" href="#19-diagram-arsitektur"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<p dir="auto">Alur cart di frontend (tidak ada cart server): <code>CustomerInput</code> dirakit client,
dikirim ke <code>POST /api/v1/pesanan</code>, lalu server menghitung ulang lewat
<code>PricingServices → PricingCalculator</code>.</p>
